###### 2025.7.29
### 一个不等式$n$元推广
https://tieba.baidu.com/p/9638505183
设 $n\ge 2, x_i>0, x_{n+1}=x_1$，猜有
$$
\left( \sum_{i=1}^n{\frac{x_{i}^{2}}{x_{i+1}}} \right) \left( \sum_{i=1}^n{\frac{x_{i+1}^{2}}{x_i}} \right) -\left( \sum_{i=1}^n{x_i} \right) ^2\ge C\left( \sum_{i=1}^n{x_{i}^{2}}-\frac{1}{n}\left( \sum_{i=1}^n{x_i} \right) ^2 \right) 
$$
其中
$$
C=\begin{cases}
	\dfrac{3}{8}\left( 11+5\sqrt{5}+\sqrt{302+150\sqrt{5}} \right) , n=3\\
	4n\left( 1-\cos \left( \dfrac{2\pi}{n} \right) \right) , n\ge 2\land n\ne 3\\
\end{cases}
$$
---

对于 $n=2$，等价于$\dfrac{\left(x_1-x_2\right){}^4 \left(x_1^2+4 x_2 x_1+x_2^2\right)}{x_1^2 x_2^2}\ge 0$，成立.

对于 $n=3$，相等于证明
$$
x^2y^2z^2\left( \left( \frac{x^2}{y}+\frac{z^2}{x}+\frac{y^2}{z} \right) \left( \frac{x^2}{z}+\frac{y^2}{x}+\frac{z^2}{y} \right) -(x+y+z)^2 \right) 
$$
$$
\ge \frac{3}{8}\left( 11+5\sqrt{5}+\sqrt{302+150\sqrt{5}} \right) x^2y^2z^2\left( x^2+y^2+z^2-\frac{1}{3}(x+y+z)^2 \right) 
$$
$LHS-RHS=$
$$
\sum_{\mathrm{sym}}{\left( \begin{array}{c}
	(y-z)^2\left( \dfrac{1}{8}\left( 1+\sqrt{5}+4\sqrt{\dfrac{3\sqrt{5}}{8}-\dfrac{1}{8}} \right) x^2yz+\dfrac{1}{2}xyz^2 \right) \left( \dfrac{1}{4}\left( -3-\sqrt{5}-2\sqrt{\dfrac{3\sqrt{5}}{2}-\dfrac{1}{2}} \right) x+y+z \right) ^2+\\
	\dfrac{1}{4}x^2(y-z)^2\left( x(y+z)+\dfrac{1}{2}\left( -1-\sqrt{3+2\sqrt{5}} \right) yz \right) ^2\\
\end{array} \right)}\ge 0.
$$
